e31368cc3d

cda37a9

logging file default almlog
  accept on
$
logging file default cmdlog
  buffer 1000
$
logging file default netclog
  accept on
$
logging file default snmplog
  accept on
$
logging file default srvlog
  accept on
  interval 10
$
logging file default weblog
  accept on
$
logging snmp
  accept on
  match cmdlog
$
!</alarm>
!<snmp>
snmp-server community FRIENDS showclear view AllView ro 
snmp-server community friends* showclear view AllView ro 
snmp-server community TESTE showclear view AllView ro 
snmp-server community TESTE1 showclear view AllView rw 
snmp-server community FRIENDS1 showclear view AllView rw 
snmp-server enable trap snmp
snmp-server enable trap bgp
